+            drawable.setStroke(dp(strokeWidth), strokeColor);
+        }

+            new IpCard("star-kitchen", "星际小食堂", "治愈", "86", "动画短片", "太空、料理、伙伴羁绊", "低门槛亲子向设定，卡牌周边和短视频切片都很自然。"),
+            new IpCard("iron-bloom", "铁花纪元", "科幻", "95", "长篇小说", "机甲、女性群像、文明重建", "世界观厚，适合做设定集、模型和战棋衍生。"),
+            new IpCard("moon-market", "月港夜市", "奇幻", "88", "原创企划", "东方幻想、摊主故事、收集图鉴", "每张摊位卡都能扩成角色小传，feed 收藏感强。"),
+            new IpCard("pixel-runners", "像素飞奔队", "运动", "79", "游<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2723" />+        } else {
+            collectedIds.add(id);
+        }
+        preferences.edit().putStringSet(KEY_COLLECTED, new HashSet<>(collectedIds)).apply();
+        renderFeed();
+    }
+
+    private LinearLayout.LayoutParams feedParams() {
+        LinearLayout.LayoutParams params = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
+                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
+                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
+        );
+        params.bottomMargin = dp(12);
+        return params;
+    }
+
+    private TextView text(String value, int sp, int color, int style) {
+        TextView view = new TextView(this);
+        view.setText(value);
+        view.setTextSize(sp);
+        view.setTextColor(color);
+        view.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT, style);
+        view.setIncludeFontPadding(true);
+        return view;
+    }
+
+    private GradientDrawable round(int color, int radius, int strokeColor, int strokeWidth) {
+        GradientDrawable drawable = new GradientDrawable();
+        drawable.setColor(color);
+        drawable.setCornerRadius(radius);
+        if (strokeWidth > 0) {
+            drawable.setStroke(dp(strokeWidth), strokeColor);
+        }
+        return drawable;
+    }
+
+    private int dp(int value) {
+        return Math.round(value * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
+    }
+
+    private static final class IpCard {
+        final String id;
+        final String title;
+        final String category;
+        final String heat;
+        final String source;
+        final String tags;
+        final String summary;
+
+        IpCard(String id, String title, String category, String heat, String source, String tags, String summary) {
+            this.id = id;
+            this.title = title;
+            this.category = category;
+            this.heat = heat;
+            this.source = source;
+            this.tags = tags;
+            this.summary = summary;
+        }
+    }

[222] assistant: 已按“本地 UI skill”的方向优化了 Android 原生界面交互。当前没有专门的 Android UI 构建 skill，所以我用了 `android-emulator-qa` 的交互验证思路，并借用 UI 产品化准则来改造原生界面。

主要改动在 [MainActivity.java](/Users/vincet/Documents/New%20project%202/app/src/main/java/com/example/ipcardcollector/MainActivity.java)：

- 新增顶部深色信息区：收藏进度、高潜数量、类型数量
- 搜索框升级：增加“清除”按钮，搜索范围包含标题、标签、衍生方向和判断依据
- 新增固定底部模式栏：`发现 / 高潜 / 收藏`
- Feed 卡片视觉升级：首字视觉块、潜力分、状态 pill、衍生方向 pill
- 点击卡片打开详情弹层，展示 IP 判断依据
- 收藏 / 取消收藏增加 Toast 反馈、按钮缩放反馈、顶部计数即时刷新
- 收藏页无内容时有空状态和“回到发现”动作
- README 同步了新交互说明：[README.md](/Users/vincet/Documents/New%20project%202/README.md)

验证：`javac` 主 Activity 编译通过，`aapt2 compile` 资源编译通过。完整 Gradle APK 构建仍受当前沙盒里的 Gradle 本地 socket 权限影响，和上次一样无法在这里完成最终 APK 生成。

[222] assistant: 已按“本地 UI skill”的方向优化了 Android 原生界面交互。当前没有专门的 Android UI 构建 skill，所以我用了 `android-emulator-qa` 的交互验证思路，并借用 UI 产品化准则来改造原生界面。

主要改动在 [MainActivity.java](/Users/vincet/Documents/New%20project%202/app/src/main/java/com/example/ipcardcollector/MainActivity.java)：

- 新增顶部深色信息区：收藏进度、高潜数量、类型数量
- 搜索框升级：增加“清除”按钮，搜索范围包含标题、标签、衍生方向和判断依据
- 新增固定底部模式栏：`发现 / 高潜 / 收藏`
- Feed 卡片视觉升级：首字视觉块、潜力分、状态 pill、衍生方向 pill
- 点击卡片打开详情弹层，展示 IP 判断依据
- 收藏 / 取消收藏增加 Toast 反馈、按钮缩放反馈、顶部计数即时刷新
- 收藏页无内容时有空状态和“回到发现”动作
- README 同步了新交互说明：[README.md](/Users/vincet/Documents/New%20project%202/README.md)

验证：`javac` 主 Activity 编译通过，`aapt2 compile` 资源编译通过。完整 Gradle APK 构建仍受当前沙盒里的 Gradle 本地 socket 权限影响，和上次一样无法在这里完成最终 APK 生成。

    private TextView text(String value, int sp, int color, int style) {
        TextView view = new TextView(this);
        view.setText(value);
        view.setTextSize(sp);
        view.setTextColor(color);
        view.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT, style);
        view.setIncludeFontPadding(true);
        return view;
    }

    private GradientDrawable round(int color, int radius, int strokeColor, int strokeWidth) {
        GradientDrawable drawable = new GradientDrawable();
        drawable.setColor(color);
        drawable.setCornerRadius(radius);
        if (strokeWidth > 0) {
            drawable.setStroke(dp(strokeWidth), strokeColor);
        }
        return drawable;
    }

    private int dp(int value) {
        return Math.round(value * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
    }

    private static final class IpCard {
        final String id;
        final String title;
        final String category;
        final int heat;
        final String source;
        final String tags;
        final String summary;
        final String direction;
        final String reason;

        IpCard(String id, String title, String category, int heat, String source, String tags, String summary, String direction, String reason) {
            this.id = id;
            this.title = title;
            this.category = category;
            this.heat = heat;
            this.source = source;
            this.tags = tags;
            this.summary = summary;
            this.direction = direction;
            this.reason = reason;
        }
    }

    private void renderTabs() {
        tabs.removeAllViews();
        addTab("发现", "全部卡片");
        addTab("高潜", "90 分以上");
        addTab("收藏", "已收集");

    private void addTab(String mode, String label) {
        boolean selected = selectedMode.equals(mode);
        TextView tab = text(mode + "\n" + label, 12, selected ? Color.WHITE : MUTED, Typeface.BOLD);
        tab.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
        tab.setLines(2);
        tab.setBackground(round(selected ? INK : Color.rgb(246, 248, 249), dp(16), Color.TRANSPARENT, 0));
        tab.setContentDescription("切换到" + mode + "模式");
        tab.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            selectedMode = mode;
            renderTabs();
            renderFeed();
        });
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams params = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(52), 1);
        params.leftMargin = dp(4);
        params.rightMargin = dp(4);
        tabs.addView(tab, params);
 <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2767" />;
        params.leftMargin = margin;
        return params;

    private LinearLayout.LayoutParams metricParams() {
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams params = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(58), 1);
        params.rightMargin = dp(8);
        return params;
    }

    private LinearLayout.LayoutParams metricParamsNoRight() {
        return new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(58), 1);
    }

    private TextView text(String value, int sp, int color, int style) {
        TextView view = new TextView(this);
        view.setText(value);
        view.setTextSize(sp);
        view.setTextColor(color);
        view.setTypeface(Typeface.DEFAULT, style);
        view.setIncludeFontPadding(true);
        return view;
    }

    private GradientDrawable round(int color, int radius, int strokeColor, int strokeWidth) {
        GradientDrawable drawable = new GradientDrawable();
        drawable.setColor(color);
        drawable.setCornerRadius(radius);
        if (strokeWidth > 0) {
            drawable.setStroke(dp(strokeWidth), strokeColor);
        }
        return drawable;
    }

    private int dp(int value) {
        return Math.round(value * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density);
    }

    private static final class IpCard {
        final String id;
        final String title;
        final String category;
        final int heat;
        final String source;
        final String tags;
        final String summary;
        final String direction;
        final String reason;

        IpCard(String id, String title, String category, int heat, String source, String tags, String summary, String direction, String reason) {
            this.id = id;
            this.title = title;
            this.category = category;
            this.heat = heat;
            this.source = source;
            this.tags = tags;
            this.summary = summary;
            this.direction = direction;
            this.reason = reason;
        }
    }
}

[456] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Add File: ../../../../private/tmp/imagegen-skill/SKILL.md
+---
+name: imagegen
+description: Generate or edit raster images when a task needs AI-created bitmap visuals such as character cards, illustrations, textures, sprites, mockups, photos, or transparent-background cutouts. Use when creating new images, transforming existing images, or deriving visual variants from references.
+---
+
+# Imagegen
+
+Use this skill whenever the user asks to generate, edit, or integrate image assets.
+
+## When to Use
+
+- Create new raster assets: character art, card art, icons, illustrations, backgrounds, UI mockups, product visuals, sprites, textures, posters.
+- Edit or restyle an existing image.
+- Generate multiple coordinated assets for an app, game, website, or deck.
+- Turn generated images into project assets, such as Android `drawable-nodpi`, web `public/assets`, or design references.
+
+## Workflow
+
+1. Translate the request into a concrete visual brief:
+   - subject and purpose
+   - style and mood
+   - number of images or variants
+   - aspect ratio or layout
+   - any text restrictions
+   - how the asset will be used in the project
+2. Call the image generation tool with the brief.
+3. Generated images are saved by default under:
+
+   ```text
+   /Users/vincet/.codex/generated_images/<generation_id>/
+   ```
+
+4. Leave originals in place. If the image is needed in a project, copy it into the project asset directory.
+5. For apps, verify the asset renders in the actual UI after integration.
+
+## Android Asset Notes
+
+- Put full-resolution generated PNGs in:
+
+  ```text
+  app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/
+  ```
+
+- Reference them as:
+
+  ```java
+  R.drawable.asset_name
+  ```
+
+- For card collections, prefer one image per card series and show:
+  - a small thumbnail in the feed
+  - a larger card image in the detail view
+
+## Quality Checklist
+
+- The asset matches the requested subject and series.
+- The image is not blurry, cropped awkwardly, or text-dependent unless text was requested.
+- Related assets share a coherent style.
+- Project copies use safe lowercase resource names.
+- The implementation was rebuilt and visually checked after adding the asset.
*** End Patch

+---
+name: imagegen
+description: Generate or edit raster images when a task needs AI-created bitmap visuals such as character cards, illustrations, textures, sprites, mockups, photos, or transparent-background cutouts. Use when creating new images, transforming existing images, or deriving visual variants from references.
+---
+
+# Imagegen
+
+Use this skill whenever the user asks to generate, edit, or integrate image assets.
+
+## When to Use
+
+- Create new raster assets: character art, card art, icons, illustrations, backgrounds, UI mockups, product visuals, sprites, textures, posters.
+- Edit or restyle an existing image.
+- Generate multiple coordinated assets for an app, game, website, or deck.
+- Turn generated images into project assets, such as Android `drawable-nodpi`, web `public/assets`, or design references.
+
+## Workflow
+
+1. Translate the request into a concrete visual brief:
+   - subject and purpose
+   - style and mood
+   - number of images or variants
+   - aspect ratio or layout
+   - any text restrictions
+   - how the asset will be used in the project
+2. Call the image generation tool with the brief.
+3. Generated images are saved by default under:
+
+   ```text
+   /Users/vincet/.codex/generated_images/<generation_id>/
+   ```
+
+4. Leave originals in place. If the image is needed in a project, copy it into the project asset directory.
+5. For apps, verify the asset renders in the actual UI after integration.
+
+## Android Asset Notes
+
+- Put full-resolution generated PNGs in:
+
+  ```text
+  app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/
+  ```
+
+- Reference them as:
+
+  ```java
+  R.drawable.asset_name
+  ```
+
+- For card collections, prefer one image per card series and show:
+  - a small thumbnail in the feed
+  - a larger card image in the detail view
+
+## Quality Checklist
+
+- The asset matches the requested subject and series.
+- The image is not blurry, cropped awkwardly, or text-dependent unless text was requested.
+- Related assets share a coherent style.
+- Project copies use safe lowercase resource names.
+- The implementation was rebuilt and visually checked after adding the asset.
*** End Patch

---
name: imagegen
description: Generate or edit raster images when a task needs AI-created bitmap visuals such as character cards, illustrations, textures, sprites, mockups, photos, or transparent-background cutouts. Use when creating new images, transforming existing images, or deriving visual variants from references.
---

# Imagegen

Use this skill whenever the user asks to generate, edit, or integrate image assets.

## When to Use

- Create new raster assets: character art, card art, icons, illustrations, backgrounds, UI mockups, product visuals, sprites, textures, posters.
- Edit or restyle an existing image.
- Generate multiple coordinated assets for an app, game, website, or deck.
- Turn generated images into project assets, such as Android `drawable-nodpi`, web `public/assets`, or design references.

## Workflow

1. Translate the request into a concrete visual brief:
   - subject and purpose
   - style and mood
   - number of images or variants
   - aspect ratio or layout
   - any text restrictions
   - how the asset will be used in the project
2. Call the image generation tool with the brief.
3. Generated images are saved by default under:

   ```text
   /Users/vincet/.codex/generated_images/<generation_id>/
   ```

4. Leave originals in place. If the image is needed in a project, copy it into the project asset directory.
5. For apps, verify the asset renders in the actual UI after integration.

## Android Asset Notes

- Put full-resolution generated PNGs in:

  ```text
  app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/
  ```

- Reference them as:

  ```java
  R.drawable.asset_name
  ```

- For card collections, prefer one image per card series and show:
  - a small thumbnail in the feed
  - a larger card image in the detail view

## Quality Checklist

- The asset matches the requested subject and series.
- The image is not blurry, cropped awkwardly, or text-dependent unless text was requested.
- Related assets share a coherent style.
- Project copies use safe lowercase resource names.
- The implementation was rebuilt and visually checked after adding the asset.

---
name: imagegen
description: Generate or edit raster images when a task needs AI-created bitmap visuals such as character cards, illustrations, textures, sprites, mockups, photos, or transparent-background cutouts. Use when creating new images, transforming existing images, or deriving visual variants from references.
---

# Imagegen

Use this skill whenever the user asks to generate, edit, or integrate image assets.

## When to Use

- Create new raster assets: character art, card art, icons, illustrations, backgrounds, UI mockups, product visuals, sprites, textures, posters.
- Edit or restyle an existing image.
- Generate multiple coordinated assets for an app, game, website, or deck.
- Turn generated images into project assets, such as Android `drawable-nodpi`, web `public/assets`, or design references.

## Workflow

1. Translate the request into a concrete visual brief:
   - subject and purpose
   - style and mood
   - number of images or variants
   - aspect ratio or layout
   - any text restrictions
   - how the asset will be used in the project
2. Call the image generation tool with the brief.
3. Generated images are saved by default under:

   ```text
   /Users/vincet/.codex/generated_images/<generation_id>/
   ```

4. Leave originals in place. If the image is needed in a project, copy it into the project asset directory.
5. For apps, verify the asset renders in the actual UI after integration.

## Android Asset Notes

- Put full-resolution generated PNGs in:

  ```text
  app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/
  ```

- Reference them as:

  ```java
  R.drawable.asset_name
  ```

- For card collections, prefer one image per card series and show:
  - a small thumbnail in the feed
  - a larger card image in the detail view

## Quality Checklist

- The asset matches the requested subject and series.
- The image is not blurry, cropped awkwardly, or text-dependent unless text was requested.
- Related assets share a coherent style.
- Project copies use safe lowercase resource names.
- The implementation was rebuilt and visually checked after adding the asset.

+                    ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,

+            if (i + 1 < card.galleryRes.length) {
+                row.addView(characterCard(card, i + 1), galleryItemParams(false));
+            } else {
+                row.addView(new View(this), galleryItemParams(false));
+            }
+        }
+        return gallery;
+    }
+
+    private View characterCard(IpCard card, int index) {

+        item.setBackground(round(Color.rgb(248, 246, 241), dp(16), LINE, 1));

+        return item;
+    }

+        } else {
+            params.leftMargin = dp(6);
+        }
